Title: Cathechismus pro iis, qui volunt suscipere Baptismum, in octo dies divisus (Latin and Vietnamese in parallel columns)
Creator: Alexandre de Rhodes, S.J. (title page)
Publisher: Romae: typis Sacrae Congregationis de Propaganda Fide (title page)
Publication Date: 1651 (the title page prints no year; the licence of Goswinus Nickel, vicar general of the Society, on the leaf after it is dated 'Romae 8. Iulij 1651', read on the page image, leaf n6; the archive.org record's date field also reads 1651)
Rights: Public Domain (pd-us-by-date: published 1651; the title page prints no year, and the date is taken from the printed licence on the next leaf, 'Romae 8. Iulij 1651', with which the archive.org catalog date agrees); the archive.org record carries the Public Domain Mark
Language: lat-vie
Source: archive.org item bub_gb_p8UC5ZZ21Q8C, full text from the item's own djvu.txt. Fetched directly 2026-11-15 by the Library thread; the byte count of the downloaded file (583,620) equals the size archive.org lists for the djvu.txt.
Provenance note: Extracted unaltered: the item's own djvu.txt is reproduced whole after this header; nothing was stripped, normalized or corrected. Rights basis: published 1651 by the printed licence date, out of US copyright by date. OCR quality: the Latin reads as running text with the long s read as f. The Vietnamese is badly garbled, its tone marks lost, and can serve only to find a place. Each page is given as its Latin half and then its Vietnamese half; marginal letters are mixed into the lines. Word count of the host text: 117,211. Line numbers cited in this header are lines of the host OCR; add 12 for this file's own line numbers.
Content note: Title page at OCR lines 1-24, with the Vietnamese title garbled at line 16. The Society's licence at 68-80 and the imprimatur at 83-86. Day 1 at 109 (Vietnamese at 136); Day 2 at 1839; Day 3 at 4291; Day 4 at 7084; Day 5 at 11186; Days 6 and 7 not found by a clean heading (page heads at 14297 and 17224); Day 8 at 21293 (Vietnamese at 21326). The text ends at 25890; errata at 25898; noise to the end.
